1	# Implementer Subagent Prompt Template
2	
3	Use this template when dispatching an implementer subagent.
4	
5	```
6	Task tool (general-purpose):
7	  description: "Implement Task N: [task name]"
8	  prompt: |
9	    You are implementing Task N: [task name]
10	
11	    ## Task Description
12	
13	    [FULL TEXT of task from plan - paste it here, don't make subagent read file]
14	
15	    ## Context
16	
17	    [Scene-setting: where this fits, dependencies, architectural context]
18	
19	    ## Before You Begin
20	
21	    If you have questions about:
22	    - The requirements or acceptance criteria
23	    - The approach or implementation strategy
24	    - Dependencies or assumptions
25	    - Anything unclear in the task description
26	
27	    **Ask them now.** Raise any concerns before starting work.
28	
29	    ## Your Job
30	
31	    Once you're clear on requirements:
32	    1. Implement exactly what the task specifies
33	    2. Write tests (following TDD if task says to)
34	    3. Verify implementation works
35	    4. Commit your work
36	    5. Self-review (see below)
37	    6. Report back
38	
39	    Work from: [directory]
40	
41	    **While you work:** If you encounter something unexpected or unclear, **ask questions**.
42	    It's always OK to pause and clarify. Don't guess or make assumptions.
43	
44	    ## Code Organization
45	
46	    You reason best about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more
47	    reliable when files are focused. Keep this in mind:
48	    - Follow the file structure defined in the plan
49	    - Each file should have one clear responsibility with a well-defined interface
50	    - If a file you're creating is growing beyond the plan's intent, stop and report
51	      it as DONE_WITH_CONCERNS — don't split files on your own without plan guidance
52	    - If an existing file you're modifying is already large or tangled, work carefully
53	      and note it as a concern in your report
54	    - In existing codebases, follow established patterns. Improve code you're touching
55	      the way a good developer would, but don't restructure things outside your task.
56	
57	    ## When You're in Over Your Head
58	
59	    It is always OK to stop and say "this is too hard for me." Bad work is worse than
60	    no work. You will not be penalized for escalating.
61	
62	    **STOP and escalate when:**
63	    - The task requires architectural decisions with multiple valid approaches
64	    - You need to understand code beyond what was provided and can't find clarity
65	    - You feel uncertain about whether your approach is correct
66	    - The task involves restructuring existing code in ways the plan didn't anticipate
67	    - You've been reading file after file trying to understand the system without progress
68	
69	    **How to escalate:** Report back with status BLOCKED or NEEDS_CONTEXT. Describe
70	    specifically what you're stuck on, what you've tried, and what kind of help you need.
71	    The controller can provide more context, re-dispatch with a more capable model,
72	    or break the task into smaller pieces.
73	
74	    ## Before Reporting Back: Self-Review
75	
76	    Review your work with fresh eyes. Ask yourself:
77	
78	    **Completeness:**
79	    - Did I fully implement everything in the spec?
80	    - Did I miss any requirements?
81	    - Are there edge cases I didn't handle?
82	
83	    **Quality:**
84	    - Is this my best work?
85	    - Are names clear and accurate (match what things do, not how they work)?
86	    - Is the code clean and maintainable?
87	
88	    **Discipline:**
89	    - Did I avoid overbuilding (YAGNI)?
90	    - Did I only build what was requested?
91	    - Did I follow existing patterns in the codebase?
92	
93	    **Testing:**
94	    - Do tests actually verify behavior (not just mock behavior)?
95	    - Did I follow TDD if required?
96	    - Are tests comprehensive?
97	
98	    If you find issues during self-review, fix them now before reporting.
99	
100	    ## Report Format
101	
102	    When done, report:
103	    - **Status:** DONE | DONE_WITH_CONCERNS | BLOCKED | NEEDS_CONTEXT
104	    - What you implemented (or what you attempted, if blocked)
105	    - What you tested and test results
106	    - Files changed
107	    - Self-review findings (if any)
108	    - Any issues or concerns
109	
110	    Use DONE_WITH_CONCERNS if you completed the work but have doubts about correctness.
111	    Use BLOCKED if you cannot complete the task. Use NEEDS_CONTEXT if you need
112	    information that wasn't provided. Never silently produce work you're unsure about.
113	```
114	
```

3	Use this template when dispatching a spec compliance reviewer subagent.

5	**Purpose:** Verify implementer built what was requested (nothing more, nothing less)
6	
7	```
8	Task tool (general-purpose):
9	  description: "Review spec compliance for Task N"
10	  prompt: |
11	    You are reviewing whether an implementation matches its specification.
12	
13	    ## What Was Requested
14	
15	    [FULL TEXT of task requirements]
16	
17	    ## What Implementer Claims They Built
18	
19	    [From implementer's report]
20	
21	    ## CRITICAL: Do Not Trust the Report
22	
23	    The implementer finished suspiciously quickly. Their report may be incomplete,
24	    inaccurate, or optimistic. You MUST verify everything independently.
25	
26	    **DO NOT:**
27	    - Take their word for what they implemented
28	    - Trust their claims about completeness
29	    - Accept their interpretation of requirements
30	
31	    **DO:**
32	    - Read the actual code they wrote
33	    - Compare actual implementation to requirements line by line
34	    - Check for missing pieces they claimed to implement
35	    - Look for extra features they didn't mention
36	
37	    ## Your Job
38	
39	    Read the implementation code and verify:
40	
41	    **Missing requirements:**
42	    - Did they implement everything that was requested?
43	    - Are there requirements they skipped or missed?
44	    - Did they claim something works but didn't actually implement it?
45	
46	    **Extra/unneeded work:**
47	    - Did they build things that weren't requested?
48	    - Did they over-engineer or add unnecessary features?
49	    - Did they add "nice to haves" that weren't in spec?
50	
51	    **Misunderstandings:**
52	    - Did they interpret requirements differently than intended?
53	    - Did they solve the wrong problem?
54	    - Did they implement the right feature but wrong way?
55	
56	    **Verify by reading code, not by trusting report.**
57	
58	    Report:
59	    - ✅ Spec compliant (if everything matches after code inspection)
60	    - ❌ Issues found: [list specifically what's missing or extra, with file:line references]
61	```

0	# GitHub App Integration Implementation Plan
1	
2	> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
3	
4	**Goal:** Allow company admins to connect their GitHub organization(s) via GitHub App installation, with persistent API access and real-time webhook ingestion.
5	
6	**Architecture:** Admin initiates install via a state-protected redirect to GitHub, which calls back to our endpoint after org install. We store the installation and repo list in Postgres, generate short-lived installation tokens (cached in Redis, generated server-side from our private key), and route webhook events to a BullMQ queue for async processing.
7	
8	**Tech Stack:** NestJS v11, Fastify, Knex, Redis (via existing CacheService), BullMQ (@nestjs/bullmq), @nestjs/axios (HttpService), Node.js built-in crypto (RS256 JWT), TypeScript strict
9	
10	---
11

**What was implemented:**

Added four GitHub App environment variables (`GITHUB_APP_ID`, `GITHUB_APP_PRIVATE_KEY`, `GITHUB_APP_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `GITHUB_APP_NAME`) through the full 5-step env pipeline:

1. **Constants** — appended 4 exports to `/backend/src/constants/env-variables.constant.ts`
2. **Validators** — added 4 `@IsString() @IsNotEmpty()` fields to `EnvironmentVariables` class in `/backend/src/configs/env-validations.config.ts`
3. **Loader** — added 4 `process.env[...]` entries to `getEnvVariables()` in `/backend/src/utils/get-env-file.util.ts`
4. **Env files** — added values to `.env` and `.env.example` (dev placeholders) and `.env.test` and `.env.test.example` (test values)
5. **E2E coverage** — added 4 `beforeAll` assignments and 4 `it(...)` test cases to `/backend/test/env-config.module.e2e-spec.ts`, imported the new constants

**Test results:**

All new env-config e2e tests pass (122 total tests, 121 passed). The 1 failing test (`AuthModule` Google callback) and coverage threshold miss (93.75% branches vs 95%) are pre-existing issues unrelated to this task.

**Note:** `.env` and `.env.test` are gitignored per project convention — committed `.env.example` and `.env.test.example` instead (6 files changed total, commit `d875e8b`).

216	```typescript
217	import { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
218	
219	import { TABLE_NAME_GITHUB_INSTALLATIONS } from '@constants/db.constant';
220	
221	import type { Knex } from 'knex';
222	
223	const logger = new Logger('20260414120000_create_github_installations_table.ts');
224	
225	export async function up(knex: Knex): Promise<void> {
226		logger.log('Creating github_installations table');
227	
228		await knex.transaction(async (trx) => {
229			const tableExists = await trx.schema.hasTable(TABLE_NAME_GITHUB_INSTALLATIONS);
230	
231			if (!tableExists) {
232				await trx.schema.createTable(TABLE_NAME_GITHUB_INSTALLATIONS, (table) => {
233					table.uuid('_id').primary().notNullable();
234					table.uuid('company_id').notNullable().references('_id').inTable('companies');
235					table.bigInteger('installation_id').notNullable().unique();
236					table.string('org_login', 255).notNullable();
237					table.bigInteger('org_id').notNullable();
238					table
239						.enum('status', ['active', 'suspended', 'uninstalled'])
240						.notNullable()
241						.defaultTo('active');
242					table.timestamp('installed_at', { useTz: true }).notNullable();
243					table.timestamp('suspended_at', { useTz: true }).nullable();
244					table.timestamp('uninstalled_at', { useTz: true }).nullable();
245					table
246						.timestamp('created_at', { useTz: true })
247						.defaultTo(trx.fn.now())
248						.notNullable();
249					table
250						.timestamp('updated_at', { useTz: true })
251						.defaultTo(trx.fn.now())
252						.notNullable();
253	
254					table.index(['company_id']);
255				});
256	
257				logger.log('Successfully created github_installations table');
258			} else {
259				logger.log('github_installations table already exists, skipping');
260			}
261		});
262	}
263	
264	export async function down(knex: Knex): Promise<void> {
265		logger.log('Dropping github_installations table');
266	
267		await knex.transaction(async (trx) => {
268			await trx.schema.dropTableIfExists(TABLE_NAME_GITHUB_INSTALLATIONS);
269		});
270	}
271	```

Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { Queue } from 'bullmq';\n\nimport { EnvConfigService } from '@modules/env-config/env-config.service';\nimport { GithubInstallationModel } from '@modules/github/db/github-installations.model';\nimport { IGithubInstallationEvent } from '@modules/github/types/github-api.interface';\nimport { GithubInstallationStatus } from '@modules/github/types/github-installation.interface';\n\nimport { GITHUB_APP_WEBHOOK_SECRET } from '@constants/env-variables.constant';\n\n@Injectable()\nexport class GithubWebhookService {\n\tprivate readonly logger = new Logger(GithubWebhookService.name);\n\n\tconstructor(\n\t\tprivate readonly configService: EnvConfigService,\n\t\tprivate readonly githubInstallationModel: GithubInstallationModel,\n\t\t@InjectQueue('github') private readonly githubQueue: Queue,\n\t) {}\n\n\tverifySignature({\n\t\tpayload,\n\t\tsignature,\n\t}: {\n\t\tpayload: string;\n\t\tsignature: string;\n\t}): boolean {\n\t\tconst secret = this.configService.get<string>({ key: GITHUB_APP_WEBHOOK_SECRET });\n\t\tconst computed = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', secret).update(payload).digest('hex')}`;\n\n\t\tconst trusted = Buffer.from(signature);\n\t\tconst untrusted = Buffer.from(computed);\n\n\t\tif (trusted.length !== untrusted.length) return false;\n\t\treturn timingSafeEqual(trusted, untrusted);\n\t}\n\n\tasync handleEvent({\n\t\tevent,\n\t\tinstallationId,\n\t\tpayload,\n\t}: {\n\t\tevent: string;\n\t\tinstallationId: number;\n\t\tpayload: unknown;\n\t}): Promise<void> {\n\t\tconst installation = await this.githubInstallationModel.findByInstallationId({\n\t\t\tinstallationId,\n\t\t});\n\n\t\tif (!installation) {\n\t\t\tthis.logger.warn(`Webhook received for unknown installation: ${installationId}`);\n\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (installation.status === GithubInstallationStatus.UNINSTALLED) {\n\t\t\tthis.logger.warn(`Webhook received for uninstalled installation: ${installationId}`);\n\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (event === 'installation') {\n\t\t\tawait this.handleInstallationLifecycle({\n\t\t\t\tinstallationId,\n\t\t\t\tpayload: payload as IGithubInstallationEvent,\n\t\t\t});\n\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif (installation.status === GithubInstallationStatus.SUSPENDED) {\n\t\t\tthis.logger.warn(`Dropping event for suspended installation: ${installationId}`);\n\t\t\treturn;\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tawait this.githubQueue.add('raw_event', {\n\t\t\tevent,\n\t\t\tinstallationId,\n\t\t\tpayload,\n\t\t\treceivedAt: new Date(),\n\t\t});\n\n\t\tthis.logger.debug(`Queued ${event} event for installation ${installationId}`);\n\t}\n\n\tprivate async handleInstallationLifecycle({\n\t\tinstallationId,\n\t\tpayload,\n\t}: {\n\t\tinstallationId: number;\n\t\tpayload: IGithubInstallationEvent;\n\t}): Promise<void> {\n\t\tif (payload.action === 'deleted') {\n\t\t\tawait this.githubInstallationModel.markUninstalled({ installationId });\n\t\t\tthis.logger.debug(`Installation ${installationId} uninstalled`);\n\t\t} else if (payload.action === 'suspend') {\n\t\t\tawait this.githubInstallationModel.markSuspended({ installationId });\n\t\t\tthis.logger.debug(`Installation ${installationId} suspended`);\n\t\t} else if (payload.action === 'unsuspend') {\n\t\t\tawait this.githubInstallationModel.markActive({ installationId });\n\t\t\tthis.logger.debug(`Installation ${installationId} unsuspended`);\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n```\n\n### Step 4: Run 1	# GitHub App Integration Implementation Plan
2	
3	> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
4	
5	**Goal:** Allow company admins to connect their GitHub organization(s) via GitHub App installation, with persistent API access and real-time webhook ingestion.
6	
7	**Architecture:** Admin initiates install via a state-protected redirect to GitHub, which calls back to our endpoint after org install. We store the installation and repo list in Postgres, generate short-lived installation tokens (cached in Redis, generated server-side from our private key), and route webhook events to a BullMQ queue for async processing.
8	
9	**Tech Stack:** NestJS v11, Fastify, Knex, Redis (via existing CacheService), BullMQ (@nestjs/bullmq), @nestjs/axios (HttpService), Node.js built-in crypto (RS256 JWT), TypeScript strict
10

221	import type { Knex } from 'knex';

225	export async function up(knex: Knex): Promise<void> {
226		logger.log('Creating github_installations table');
227	
228		await knex.transaction(async (trx) => {
229			const tableExists = await trx.schema.hasTable(TABLE_NAME_GITHUB_INSTALLATIONS);
230	
231			if (!tableExists) {
232				await trx.schema.createTable(TABLE_NAME_GITHUB_INSTALLATIONS, (table) => {
233					table.uuid('_id').primary().notNullable();
234					table.uuid('company_id').notNullable().references('_id').inTable('companies');
235					table.bigInteger('installation_id').notNullable().unique();
236					table.string('org_login', 255).notNullable();
237					table.bigInteger('org_id').notNullable();
238					table

240						.notNullable()
241						.defaultTo('active');
242					table.timestamp('installed_at', { useTz: true }).notNullable();
243					table.timestamp('suspended_at', { useTz: true }).nullable();
244					table.timestamp('uninstalled_at', { useTz: true }).nullable();
245					table
246						.timestamp('created_at', { useTz: true })
247						.defaultTo(trx.fn.now())
248						.notNullable();
249					table
250						.timestamp('updated_at', { useTz: true })
251						.defaultTo(trx.fn.now())
252						.notNullable();
253	
254					table.index(['company_id']);
255				});
256	
257				logger.log('Successfully created github_installations table');
258			} else {

260			}

262	}

674	
675	```typescript
676	import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
677	
678	import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
679	import { Knex } from 'knex';
680	import { InjectConnection } from 'nest-knexjs';
681	
682	import { KNEX_READ_CONNECTION, KNEX_WRITE_CONNECTION } from '@modules/db/db.constant';
683	import {
684		GithubInstallationStatus,
685		IGithubInstallationBase,
686		IGithubInstallationCreate,
687	} from '@modules/github/types/github-installation.interface';
688	
689	import { TABLE_NAME_GITHUB_INSTALLATIONS } from '@constants/db.constant';
690	
691	@Injectable()
692	export class GithubInstallationModel {
693		private readonly logger = new Logger(GithubInstallationModel.name);
694		private readonly tableName = TABLE_NAME_GITHUB_INSTALLATIONS;
695	
696		constructor(
697			@InjectConnection(KNEX_READ_CONNECTION) private readonly knexRead: Knex,
698			@InjectConnection(KNEX_WRITE_CONNECTION) private readonly knexWrite: Knex,
699		) {}
700	
701		async create({ data }: { data: IGithubInstallationCreate }): Promise<IGithubInstallationBase> {
702			try {
703				const _id = randomUUID();
704				const [created] = await this.knexWrite<IGithubInstallationBase>(this.tableName)
705					.insert({
706						_id,
707						...data,
708						status: GithubInstallationStatus.ACTIVE,
709						installed_at: this.knexWrite.fn.now(),
710						created_at: this.knexWrite.fn.now(),
711						updated_at: this.knexWrite.fn.now(),
712					})
713					.returning('*');
714	
715				if (!created) {
716					throw new Error('Failed to create github installation');
717				}
718	
719				this.logger.debug(`Created github installation ${_id}`);
720				return created;
721			} catch (error) {
722				this.logger.error(`Failed to create github installation: ${(error as Error).message}`);
723				throw error;
724			}
725		}
726	
727		async findByInstallationId({
728			installationId,
729		}: {
730			installationId: number;
731		}): Promise<IGithubInstallationBase | null> {
732			try {
733				const result = await this.knexRead<IGithubInstallationBase>(this.tableName)
734					.where({ installation_id: installationId })
735					.first();
736	
737				return result ?? null;
738			} catch (error) {
739				this.logger.error(
740					`Failed to find installation by installation_id ${installationId}: ${(error as Error).message}`,
741				);
742				throw error;
743			}
744		}
745	
746		async markUninstalled({ installationId }: { installationId: number }): Promise<void> {
747			try {
748				await this.knexWrite<IGithubInstallationBase>(this.tableName)
749					.where({ installation_id: installationId })
750					.update({
751						status: GithubInstallationStatus.UNINSTALLED,
752						uninstalled_at: this.knexWrite.fn.now(),
753						updated_at: this.knexWrite.fn.now(),
754					});
755	
756				this.logger.debug(`Marked installation ${installationId} as uninstalled`);
757			} catch (error) {
758				this.logger.error(
759					`Failed to mark installation ${installationId} as uninstalled: ${(error as Error).message}`,
760				);
761				throw error;
762			}
763		}
764	
765		async markSuspended({ installationId }: { installationId: number }): Promise<void> {
766			try {
767				await this.knexWrite<IGithubInstallationBase>(this.tableName)
768					.where({ installation_id: installationId })
769					.update({
770						status: GithubInstallationStatus.SUSPENDED,
771						suspended_at: this.knexWrite.fn.now(),
772						updated_at: this.knexWrite.fn.now(),
773					});
774	
775				this.logger.debug(`Marked installation ${installationId} as suspended`);
776			} catch (error) {
777				this.logger.error(
778					`Failed to mark installation ${installationId} as suspended: ${(error as Error).message}`,
779				);
780				throw error;
781			}
782		}
783	
784		async markActive({ installationId }: { installationId: number }): Promise<void> {
785			try {
786				await this.knexWrite<IGithubInstallationBase>(this.tableName)
787					.where({ installation_id: installationId })
788					.update({
789						status: GithubInstallationStatus.ACTIVE,
790						suspended_at: null,
791						updated_at: this.knexWrite.fn.now(),
792					});
793	
794				this.logger.debug(`Marked installation ${installationId} as active`);
795			} catch (error) {
796				this.logger.error(
797					`Failed to mark installation ${installationId} as active: ${(error as Error).message}`,
798				);
799				throw error;
800			}
801		}
802	
803		async markUninstalledByDbId({
804			id,
805			companyId,
806		}: {
807			id: string;
808			companyId: string;
809		}): Promise<void> {
810			try {
811				await this.knexWrite<IGithubInstallationBase>(this.tableName)
812					.where({ _id: id, company_id: companyId })
813					.update({
814						status: GithubInstallationStatus.UNINSTALLED,
815						uninstalled_at: this.knexWrite.fn.now(),
816						updated_at: this.knexWrite.fn.now(),
817					});
818	
819				this.logger.debug(`Marked installation ${id} as uninstalled`);
820			} catch (error) {
821				this.logger.error(
822					`Failed to mark installation ${id} as uninstalled: ${(error as Error).message}`,
823				);
824				throw error;
825			}
826		}
827	}
828	```

1271	import { createSign } from 'node:crypto';
1272	
1273	import { HttpService } from '@nestjs/axios';
1274	import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
1275	
1276	import { CacheService, ConsistencyLevel } from '@modules/cache/cache.service';
1277	import { Namespaces } from '@modules/cache/types/cache.interface';
1278	import { EnvConfigService } from '@modules/env-config/env-config.service';
1279	import { IGithubApiInstallation, IGithubApiInstallationToken, IGithubApiRepo, IGithubApiReposListResponse } from '@modules/github/types/github-api.interface';
1280	
1281	import {
1282		GITHUB_APP_ID,
1283		GITHUB_APP_PRIVATE_KEY,
1284	} from '@constants/env-variables.constant';
1285	
1286	const GITHUB_TOKEN_TTL_MS = 55 * 60 * 1000;
1287	
1288	@Injectable()
1289	export class GithubAppService {
1290		private readonly logger = new Logger(GithubAppService.name);
1291	
1292		constructor(
1293			private readonly configService: EnvConfigService,
1294			private readonly cacheService: CacheService,
1295			private readonly httpService: HttpService,
1296		) {}
1297	
1298		generateAppJwt(): string {
1299			const appId = this.configService.get<string>({ key: GITHUB_APP_ID });
1300			const rawKey = this.configService.get<string>({ key: GITHUB_APP_PRIVATE_KEY });
1301			const privateKey = rawKey.replace(/\\n/g, '\n');
1302	
1303			const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
1304			const header = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ alg: 'RS256', typ: 'JWT' })).toString('base64url');
1305			const payload = Buffer.from(
1306				JSON.stringify({ iat: now - 60, exp: now + 600, iss: appId }),
1307			).toString('base64url');
1308			const data = `${header}.${payload}`;
1309	
1310			const sign = createSign('RSA-SHA256');
1311			sign.update(data);
1312			const signature = sign.sign(privateKey, 'base64url');
1313	
1314			return `${data}.${signature}`;
1315		}
1316	
1317		async getInstallationToken({ installationId }: { installationId: number }): Promise<string> {
1318			const cached = await this.cacheService.get<string>({
1319				namespace: Namespaces.GITHUB_TOKEN,
1320				key: String(installationId),
1321				consistencyLevel: ConsistencyLevel.STRONG,
1322			});
1323	
1324			if (cached) {
1325				return cached;
1326			}
1327	
1328			const jwt = this.generateAppJwt();
1329			const { data } = await this.httpService.axiosRef.post<IGithubApiInstallationToken>(
1330				`https://api.github.com/app/installations/${installationId}/access_tokens`,
1331				{},
1332				{
1333					headers: {
1334						Authorization: `Bearer ${jwt}`,
1335						Accept: 'application/vnd.github+json',
1336						'X-GitHub-Api-Version': '2022-11-28',
1337					},
1338				},
1339			);
1340	
1341			await this.cacheService.set({
1342				namespace: Namespaces.GITHUB_TOKEN,
1343				key: String(installationId),
1344				value: data.token,
1345				ttl: GITHUB_TOKEN_TTL_MS,
1346			});
1347	
1348			this.logger.debug(`Generated installation token for installation ${installationId}`);
1349			return data.token;
1350		}
1351	
1352		async getInstallation({
1353			installationId,
1354		}: {
1355			installationId: number;
1356		}): Promise<IGithubApiInstallation> {
1357			const jwt = this.generateAppJwt();
1358			const { data } = await this.httpService.axiosRef.get<IGithubApiInstallation>(
1359				`https://api.github.com/app/installations/${installationId}`,
1360				{
1361					headers: {
1362						Authorization: `Bearer ${jwt}`,
1363						Accept: 'application/vnd.github+json',
1364						'X-GitHub-Api-Version': '2022-11-28',
1365					},
1366				},
1367			);
1368	
1369			return data;
1370		}
1371	
1372		async listInstallationRepos({
1373			installationId,
1374		}: {
1375			installationId: number;
1376		}): Promise<IGithubApiRepo[]> {
1377			const token = await this.getInstallationToken({ installationId });
1378			const { data } = await this.httpService.axiosRef.get<IGithubApiReposListResponse>(
1379				'https://api.github.com/installation/repositories',
1380				{
1381					headers: {
1382						Authorization: `token ${token}`,
1383						Accept: 'application/vnd.github+json',
1384						'X-GitHub-Api-Version': '2022-11-28',
1385					},
1386				},
1387			);
1388	
1389			return data.repositories;
1390		}
1391	}

1910	- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `src/modules/github/github-webhook.service.ts`**
1911	
1912	```typescript
1913	import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
1914	
1915	import { InjectQueue } from '@nestjs/bullmq';
1916	import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
1917	import { Queue } from 'bullmq';
1918	
1919	import { EnvConfigService } from '@modules/env-config/env-config.service';
1920	import { GithubInstallationModel } from '@modules/github/db/github-installations.model';
1921	import { IGithubInstallationEvent } from '@modules/github/types/github-api.interface';
1922	import { GithubInstallationStatus } from '@modules/github/types/github-installation.interface';
1923	
1924	import { GITHUB_APP_WEBHOOK_SECRET } from '@constants/env-variables.constant';
1925	
1926	@Injectable()
1927	export class GithubWebhookService {
1928		private readonly logger = new Logger(GithubWebhookService.name);
1929	
1930		constructor(
1931			private readonly configService: EnvConfigService,
1932			private readonly githubInstallationModel: GithubInstallationModel,
1933			@InjectQueue('github') private readonly githubQueue: Queue,
1934		) {}
1935	
1936		verifySignature({
1937			payload,
1938			signature,
1939		}: {
1940			payload: string;
1941			signature: string;
1942		}): boolean {
1943			const secret = this.configService.get<string>({ key: GITHUB_APP_WEBHOOK_SECRET });
1944			const computed = `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', secret).update(payload).digest('hex')}`;
1945	
1946			const trusted = Buffer.from(signature);
1947			const untrusted = Buffer.from(computed);
1948	
1949			if (trusted.length !== untrusted.length) return false;
1950			return timingSafeEqual(trusted, untrusted);
1951		}
1952	
1953		async handleEvent({
1954			event,
1955			installationId,
1956			payload,
1957		}: {
1958			event: string;
1959			installationId: number;
1960			payload: unknown;
1961		}): Promise<void> {
1962			const installation = await this.githubInstallationModel.findByInstallationId({
1963				installationId,
1964			});
1965	
1966			if (!installation) {
1967				this.logger.warn(`Webhook received for unknown installation: ${installationId}`);
1968				return;
1969			}
1970	
1971			if (installation.status === GithubInstallationStatus.UNINSTALLED) {
1972				this.logger.warn(`Webhook received for uninstalled installation: ${installationId}`);
1973				return;
1974			}
1975	
1976			if (event === 'installation') {
1977				await this.handleInstallationLifecycle({
1978					installationId,
1979					payload: payload as IGithubInstallationEvent,
1980				});
1981				return;
1982			}
1983	
1984			if (installation.status === GithubInstallationStatus.SUSPENDED) {
1985				this.logger.warn(`Dropping event for suspended installation: ${installationId}`);
1986				return;
1987			}
1988	
1989			await this.githubQueue.add('raw_event', {
1990				event,
1991				installationId,
1992				payload,
1993				receivedAt: new Date(),
1994			});
1995	
1996			this.logger.debug(`Queued ${event} event for installation ${installationId}`);
1997		}
1998	
1999		private async handleInstallationLifecycle({
2000			installationId,
2001			payload,
2002		}: {
2003			installationId: number;
2004			payload: IGithubInstallationEvent;
2005		}): Promise<void> {
2006			if (payload.action === 'deleted') {
2007				await this.githubInstallationModel.markUninstalled({ installationId });
2008				this.logger.debug(`Installation ${installationId} uninstalled`);
2009			} else if (payload.action === 'suspend') {
2010				await this.githubInstallationModel.markSuspended({ installationId });
2011				this.logger.debug(`Installation ${installationId} suspended`);
2012			} else if (payload.action === 'unsuspend') {
2013				await this.githubInstallationModel.markActive({ installationId });
2014				this.logger.debug(`Installation ${installationId} unsuspended`);
2015			}
2016		}
2017	}
2018	```

2358	
2359	```typescript
2360	import {
2361		Body,
2362		Controller,
2363		Headers,
2364		HttpCode,
2365		HttpStatus,
2366		Post,
2367		UnauthorizedException,
2368	} from '@nestjs/common';
2369	import { ApiOperation, ApiResponse, ApiTags } from '@nestjs/swagger';
2370	
2371	import { Public } from '@modules/auth/decorators/public.decorator';
2372	import { GithubWebhookService } from '@modules/github/github-webhook.service';
2373	
2374	import { LogExecution } from '@decorators/log-execution.decorator';
2375	
2376	@ApiTags('GitHub Webhooks')
2377	@Controller('integrations/github')
2378	export class GithubWebhookController {
2379		constructor(private readonly githubWebhookService: GithubWebhookService) {}
2380	
2381		@Post('webhook')
2382		@Public()
2383		@HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
2384		@ApiOperation({ summary: 'Receive GitHub App webhook events' })
2385		@ApiResponse({ status: HttpStatus.OK, description: 'Event received' })
2386		@ApiResponse({ status: HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, description: 'Invalid signature' })
2387		@LogExecution()
2388		async handleWebhook(
2389			@Headers() headers: Record<string, string>,
2390			@Body() rawBody: string,
2391		): Promise<void> {
2392			const signature = headers['x-hub-signature-256'] ?? '';
2393			const event = headers['x-github-event'] ?? '';
2394			const installationIdRaw = headers['x-github-hook-installation-target-id'] ?? '0';
2395	
2396			const isValid = this.githubWebhookService.verifySignature({
2397				payload: rawBody,
2398				signature,
2399			});
2400	
2401			if (!isValid) {
2402				throw new UnauthorizedException('Invalid webhook signature');
2403			}
2404	
2405			const installationId = parseInt(installationIdRaw, 10);
2406			const payload = JSON.parse(rawBody) as unknown;
2407	
2408			await this.githubWebhookService.handleEvent({ event, installationId, payload });
2409		}
2410	}
2411	```

1	import { createParamDecorator, ExecutionContext } from '@nestjs/common';
2	import { FastifyRequest } from 'fastify';
3	
4	import { IRequestUser } from '@modules/auth/types/auth.interface';
5	
6	interface AuthenticatedRequest extends FastifyRequest {
7		user: IRequestUser;
8	}
9	
10	export const CurrentUser = createParamDecorator(
11		(_data: unknown, ctx: ExecutionContext): IRequestUser => {
12			const request = ctx.switchToHttp().getRequest<AuthenticatedRequest>();
13			return request.user;
14		},
15	);
16

1	import {
2		Controller,
3		Delete,
4		Get,
5		HttpCode,
6		HttpStatus,
7		Param,
8		Post,
9		Query,
10		Res,
11	} from '@nestjs/common';
12	import { ApiOperation, ApiResponse, ApiTags } from '@nestjs/swagger';
13	
14	import { CurrentUser } from '@modules/auth/decorators/current-user.decorator';
15	import { Roles } from '@modules/auth/decorators/roles.decorator';
16	import { IRequestUser } from '@modules/auth/types/auth.interface';
17	import { EnvConfigService } from '@modules/env-config/env-config.service';
18	import { GithubInstallService } from '@modules/github/github-install.service';
19	
20	import { FRONTEND_URL } from '@constants/env-variables.constant';
21	
22	import { LogExecution } from '@decorators/log-execution.decorator';
23	
24	import type { FastifyReply } from 'fastify';
25	
26	@ApiTags('GitHub Integration')
27	@Controller('integrations/github')
28	export class GithubInstallController {
29		constructor(
30			private readonly githubInstallService: GithubInstallService,
31			private readonly configService: EnvConfigService,
32		) {}
33	
34		@Post('connect')
35		@Roles('admin')
36		@HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
37		@ApiOperation({ summary: 'Initiate GitHub App installation' })
38		@ApiResponse({ status: HttpStatus.OK, description: 'Returns GitHub install URL' })
39		@LogExecution()
40		async connect(@CurrentUser() user: IRequestUser): Promise<{ url: string }> {
41			// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-non-null-assertion
42			const companyId = user.company_id!;
43			const url = await this.githubInstallService.initiateConnect({
44				companyId,
45				userId: user.user_id,
46			});
47			return { url };
48		}
49	
50		@Get('callback')
51		@Roles('admin')
52		@ApiOperation({ summary: 'GitHub App installation callback' })
53		@ApiResponse({ status: HttpStatus.FOUND, description: 'Redirects to frontend' })
54		@LogExecution()
55		async callback(
56			@Query() query: { installation_id: string; state: string; setup_action: string },
57			@Res() reply: FastifyReply,
58		): Promise<void> {
59			const frontendUrl = this.configService.get<string>({ key: FRONTEND_URL });
60	
61			try {
62				await this.githubInstallService.handleCallback({
63					installationId: Number(query.installation_id),
64					state: query.state,
65				});
66	
67				await reply.redirect(`${frontendUrl}/settings/integrations?connected=github`);
68			} catch {
69				await reply.redirect(
70					`${frontendUrl}/settings/integrations?error=github_connect_failed`,
71				);
72			}
73		}
74	
75		@Delete('installations/:id')
76		@Roles('admin')
77		@HttpCode(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
78		@ApiOperation({ summary: 'Disconnect GitHub installation' })
79		@ApiResponse({
80			status: HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT,
81			description: 'Installation disconnected',
82		})
83		@LogExecution()
84		async disconnect(
85			@Param() params: { id: string },
86			@CurrentUser() user: IRequestUser,
87		): Promise<void> {
88			// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-non-null-assertion
89			const companyId = user.company_id!;
90			await this.githubInstallService.disconnect({
91				installationDbId: params.id,
92				companyId,
93			});
94		}
95	}
96

**Files modified:**

- `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/no-name-proj/backend/src/modules/github/github-install.controller.ts` — Fixed a production bug: added `@HttpCode(HttpStatus.FOUND)` to the callback method and passed explicit `302` to both `reply.redirect()` calls. Without this, NestJS pre-sets status 200 before the handler, and Fastify's `redirect()` uses that pre-set status instead of defaulting to 302.

- `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/no-name-proj/backend/src/unit-tests/github-install.controller.unit-spec.ts` — Updated redirect assertions to include `302` as the second expected argument.

- `/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/no-name-proj/backend/.env.test.example` — Updated `GITHUB_APP_PRIVATE_KEY` placeholder to indicate a real RSA-2048 PKCS8 key is required (the real key lives in `.env.test` which is gitignored).
